Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in magnetics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 967 and the invariant I(A,B) = 502 mod 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the magnetics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^967 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 502-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Given a magnetics algebra A over field F with optimality condition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 547. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 289/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 547 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the magnetics structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 289. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
